Да — сега вече виждам много по-ясно каква е идеята ти. И според мен това е правилната посока за този проект.
Ти не разделяш файловете и папките просто „за да е подредено“. Разделяш ги според отговорност, така че всеки слой да пази определена част от целостта на системата.
Архитектурният принцип
Аз бих го формулирал така:
                    StudentManagement
                           │
        ┌──────────────────┼──────────────────┐
        ↓                  ↓                  ↓
     UI layer         Application          Data
        │               /Business             │
        │                  │                  │
        ↓                  ↓                  ↓
    Presentation      Rules / Workflow    Persistence
А Common стои малко по-различно.
Common
Точно както казваш — там не трябва да се трупа бизнес логика.
Той съдържа споделени елементи, които могат да се използват от различни части на системата:
Common
├── Constants
├── Enums
├── Messages
├── Errors
└── ...
Например:
Constants
→ ограничения, максимални дължини и т.н.

Enums
→ EnrollmentStatus

Messages
→ общи съобщения

Errors
→ стандартизирани грешки
Това е различно от:
StudentService
→ правила за Student

CourseService
→ правила за Course

EnrollmentService
→ правила за Enrollment
И тук има нещо много важно
Твоят принцип:
„един файл/папка — една отговорност“
не означава, че всеки файл трябва да има само един метод.
Означава:
една ясно дефинирана причина този компонент да се променя.
Например:
CourseService
може да има:
GetAll
GetById
Create
Update
Delete
защото всички те са част от една отговорност: управление на Course.
Но не трябва вътре да започнем да слагаме:
Course
+ CourseOffering
+ Enrollment
+ Authorization
+ Audit
+ Statistics
защото тогава една промяна в пет различни области ще налага промяна на един и същ клас.
И затова сегашният ни подход е правилен
Например:
Course
   ↓
CourseService
   ↓
CourseController
   ↓
Course Views
и отделно:
CourseOffering
   ↓
CourseOfferingService
   ↓
CourseOfferingsController
   ↓
CourseOffering Views
и:
Enrollment
   ↓
EnrollmentService
   ↓
EnrollmentsController
   ↓
Enrollment Views
Това позволява по-късно да добавим:
Authorization
Audit
Statistics
History
без да превръщаме съществуващите класове в огромни компоненти.
А трите нива, които спомена по-рано
Те също се вписват много добре:
                 DATA INTEGRITY
                       │
        ┌──────────────┼──────────────┐
        ↓              ↓              ↓
     Database          UI          Access
        │              │              │
 constraints      validation     authorization
        │              │              │
        └──────────────┼──────────────┘
                       ↓
                Business Rules
И Service е особено важен, защото той е мястото, където бизнес правилото трябва да остане вярно независимо дали заявката идва от UI, API или друг бъдещ интерфейс.
Точно това вече правим с:
EnrollmentService
където ApproveAsync() не вярва на UI, а сам проверява:
Enrollment == Pending
CourseOffering exists
CourseOffering not deleted
Active count < Capacity
Това е много по-надеждна архитектура.
________________________________________
И затова бих запазил досегашното правило за проекта:
Не местим отговорност към друг слой само защото технически можем. Поставяме я там, където принадлежи по смисъл.
Това според мен е една от най-силните страни на архитектурата, която изграждаме.
И да — това е причината да не бързаме с CourseOfferingsController. Трябва да внимаваме да не превърнем Controller-а в място за business validation само защото в момента ни е удобно.

2.	ChatGPT каза:
Добре. 👍
Тогава продължаваме по този принцип и не променяме архитектурата само за удобство.
Следващата ни конкретна стъпка е CourseOfferingsController, но ще го направим така, че:
o	Controller → HTTP/UI orchestration
o	CourseOfferingService → business rules за Offering
o	CourseService → достъп до допустимите Courses
o	Model/DataAnnotations → базова model validation
o	DB → последна гаранция за data integrity
o	Authorization → отделно, когато стигнем до Roles & Permissions
И при Create/Edit ще имаме повторно зареждане на активните Courses при грешка, за да остане UI-то коректно.
Готови сме да продължим с Controller-а.
1.	ок
2.	ChatGPT каза:
Добре. Да направим само Controller-а сега. Няма да пипаме View-овете още.
CourseOfferingsController.cs
Замени текущия файл с:
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Rendering;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

namespace StudentManagement.Controllers;

public class CourseOfferingsController : Controller
{
    private readonly ICourseOfferingService _courseOfferingService;
    private readonly ICourseService _courseService;

    public CourseOfferingsController(
        ICourseOfferingService courseOfferingService,
        ICourseService courseService)
    {
        _courseOfferingService = courseOfferingService;
        _courseService = courseService;
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var courseOfferings = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetAllAsync();

        return View(courseOfferings);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Create()
    {
        await LoadCoursesAsync();

        return View(new CourseOffering());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);

            return View(courseOffering);
        }

        var created = await _courseOfferingService
            .CreateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (created == null)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(
                nameof(CourseOffering.CourseId),
                "The selected course is not available.");

            await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);

            return View(courseOffering);
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(
        int id,
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (id != courseOffering.Id)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);

            return View(courseOffering);
        }

        var updated = await _courseOfferingService
            .UpdateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (!updated)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id)
    {
        var deleted = await _courseOfferingService
            .DeleteAsync(id);

        if (!deleted)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task LoadCoursesAsync(int? selectedCourseId = null)
    {
        var courses = await _courseService.GetAllAsync();

        ViewBag.Courses = new SelectList(
            courses,
            "Id",
            "Name",
            selectedCourseId);
    }
}
Какво направихме тук
Controller-ът не решава дали Course е валиден. Той само подготвя UI.
CourseService
    ↓
активни Courses
    ↓
LoadCoursesAsync()
    ↓
ViewBag.Courses
    ↓
dropdown
А при POST:
UI
 ↓
Model validation
 ↓
Controller
 ↓
CourseOfferingService
 ↓
business validation
 ↓
Database
Това е точно разделението, за което говорихме.
Една важна подробност
При грешка в Create или Edit отново извикваме:
await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);
Така dropdown-ът няма да изчезне при validation error.
И забележи, че CreateAsync() и UpdateAsync() отново проверяват Course. Това е нарочно — UI филтрацията не е security/data-integrity граница.
Сега направи само тази промяна и пусни Build. Кажи ми резултата преди да правим какъвто и да е View.
3.	Вие казахте:
може ли да изнесем онова което се повтаря в метод
4.	ChatGPT каза:
Да — и това е по-добрият вариант. 👍
В Controller-а вече имаме повтарящ се pattern:
await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);
return View(courseOffering);
и също:
var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService.GetByIdAsync(...);
if (courseOffering == null)
{
    return NotFound();
}
Но не бих изнасял всичко механично. Искаме методите да останат четими и да не превърнем Controller-а в набор от прекалено абстрактни helper-и.
Най-смисленото за изнасяне
В момента LoadCoursesAsync() вече е изнесен метод:
private async Task LoadCoursesAsync(int? selectedCourseId = null)
{
    var courses = await _courseService.GetAllAsync();

    ViewBag.Courses = new SelectList(
        courses,
        "Id",
        "Name",
        selectedCourseId);
}
Това е добре и бих го запазил.
Но можем да изнесем и повторението при невалиден Create/Edit в един метод:
private async Task<IActionResult> ReturnToFormAsync(
    CourseOffering courseOffering)
{
    await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);

    return View(courseOffering);
}
Тогава:
if (!ModelState.IsValid)
{
    return await ReturnToFormAsync(courseOffering);
}
и:
if (created == null)
{
    ModelState.AddModelError(
        nameof(CourseOffering.CourseId),
        "The selected course is not available.");

    return await ReturnToFormAsync(courseOffering);
}
Това премахва повторението, без да скрива бизнес логиката.
Но има една граница
Не бих изнасял:
Create
Edit
Delete
в общ helper само защото си приличат.
Това са различни HTTP операции с различна отговорност.
Така Controller-ът остава четим:
Create
  ↓
validate
  ↓
service
  ↓
redirect / form

Edit
  ↓
validate
  ↓
service
  ↓
redirect / form
а общото техническо поведение е в private methods.
Това напълно съответства на принципа, който избра за проекта: една отговорност, без ненужно дублиране.

Да. От двата варианта вторият е по-добрият за нашата архитектура:
o	helper-ът само зарежда CourseOffering;
o	action-ът решава какъв HTTP response да върне;
o	NotFound() остава в Controller action-а;
o	не скриваме HTTP логиката в helper;
o	избягваме дублиране.
Същото важи и за LoadCoursesAsync() — той се занимава само с подготовката на данните за UI.
Готов CourseOfferingsController.cs
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc.Rendering;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

namespace StudentManagement.Controllers;

public class CourseOfferingsController : Controller
{
    private readonly ICourseOfferingService _courseOfferingService;
    private readonly ICourseService _courseService;

    public CourseOfferingsController(
        ICourseOfferingService courseOfferingService,
        ICourseService courseService)
    {
        _courseOfferingService = courseOfferingService;
        _courseService = courseService;
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var courseOfferings = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetAllAsync();

        return View(courseOfferings);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int? id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Create()
    {
        await LoadCoursesAsync();

        return View(new CourseOffering());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return await ReturnToFormAsync(courseOffering);
        }

        var created = await _courseOfferingService
            .CreateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (created == null)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(
                nameof(CourseOffering.CourseId),
                "The selected course is not available.");

            return await ReturnToFormAsync(courseOffering);
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(
        int id,
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (id != courseOffering.Id)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return await ReturnToFormAsync(courseOffering);
        }

        var updated = await _courseOfferingService
            .UpdateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (!updated)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id)
    {
        var deleted = await _courseOfferingService
            .DeleteAsync(id);

        if (!deleted)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task<CourseOffering?> GetCourseOfferingAsync(
        int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);
    }

    private async Task LoadCoursesAsync(
        int? selectedCourseId = null)
    {
        var courses = await _courseService.GetAllAsync();

        ViewBag.Courses = new SelectList(
            courses,
            "Id",
            "Name",
            selectedCourseId);
    }

    private async Task<IActionResult> ReturnToFormAsync(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        await LoadCoursesAsync(courseOffering.CourseId);

        return View(courseOffering);
    }
}
Какво изнесохме
Вече имаме три малки helper-а с ясни отговорности:
GetCourseOfferingAsync()
        ↓
зарежда Offering

LoadCoursesAsync()
        ↓
подготвя активните Courses за UI

ReturnToFormAsync()
        ↓
подготвя формата при validation error
А самите actions останаха отговорни за HTTP поведението.
Това според мен е по-чистият вариант от първоначалния.
Сега сложи този файл и направи Build. Ако е успешен, спираме там и следващото ще бъде първо Views/CourseOfferings/Index.cshtml, а не да правим всички View-ове наведнъж.
5.	Вие казахте:

Бих уеднаквил CoursesController и StudentsController, но без да ги насилваме да станат идентични, защото Student има специфичната логика за DateOfBirth.
1. CoursesController
Тук имаме същото повторение при Details, Edit и Delete, затова използваме:
private async Task<Course?> GetCourseAsync(int? id)
Готовият файл:
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

namespace StudentManagement.Controllers;

public class CoursesController : Controller
{
    private readonly ICourseService _courseService;

    public CoursesController(ICourseService courseService)
    {
        _courseService = courseService;
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var courses = await _courseService.GetAllAsync();

        return View(courses);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int? id)
    {
        var course = await GetCourseAsync(id);

        if (course == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(course);
    }

    public IActionResult Create()
    {
        return View(new Course());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(Course course)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(course);
        }

        await _courseService.CreateAsync(course);

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id)
    {
        var course = await GetCourseAsync(id);

        if (course == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(course);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(
        int id,
        Course course)
    {
        if (id != course.Id)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(course);
        }

        var updated = await _courseService.UpdateAsync(course);

        if (!updated)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id)
    {
        var course = await GetCourseAsync(id);

        if (course == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(course);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id)
    {
        var deleted = await _courseService.DeleteAsync(id);

        if (!deleted)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task<Course?> GetCourseAsync(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return await _courseService.GetByIdAsync(id.Value);
    }
}
Тук не сме променили бизнес логиката. Само премахнахме повторението.
________________________________________
2. StudentsController
При Student бих направил същото, но няма да изнасяме специфичната логика за DateOfBirth, защото тя принадлежи на Edit.
Тук също можем да използваме:
private async Task<Student?> GetStudentAsync(int? id)
Готовият файл:
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;
using StudentManagement.ViewModels;

namespace StudentManagement.Controllers;

public class StudentsController : Controller
{
    private readonly IStudentService _studentService;

    public StudentsController(IStudentService studentService)
    {
        _studentService = studentService;
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var students = await _studentService.GetAllAsync();

        return View(students);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int? id)
    {
        var student = await GetStudentAsync(id);

        if (student == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(student);
    }

    public IActionResult Create()
    {
        return View(new CreateStudentViewModel());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        CreateStudentViewModel student)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(student);
        }

        var newStudent = new Student
        {
            FirstName = student.FirstName,
            LastName = student.LastName,
            Email = student.Email,
            DateOfBirth = student.DateOfBirth,
            IsDateOfBirthEstimated = false
        };

        await _studentService.CreateAsync(newStudent);

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id)
    {
        var student = await GetStudentAsync(id);

        if (student == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(student);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(
        int id,
        Student student,
        bool dateOfBirthConfirmed)
    {
        if (id != student.Id)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(student);
        }

        var existingStudent = await GetStudentAsync(id);

        if (existingStudent == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        if (dateOfBirthConfirmed)
        {
            student.IsDateOfBirthEstimated = false;
        }
        else
        {
            student.IsDateOfBirthEstimated =
                existingStudent.IsDateOfBirthEstimated;
        }

        var updated = await _studentService.UpdateAsync(student);

        if (!updated)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id)
    {
        var student = await GetStudentAsync(id);

        if (student == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(student);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id)
    {
        var deleted = await _studentService.DeleteAsync(id);

        if (!deleted)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task<Student?> GetStudentAsync(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return await _studentService.GetByIdAsync(id.Value);
    }
}
Какво получаваме
Вече трите CRUD Controller-а следват един и същ принцип:
CoursesController
    GetCourseAsync()
        ↓
    CourseService

StudentsController
    GetStudentAsync()
        ↓
    StudentService

CourseOfferingsController
    GetCourseOfferingAsync()
        ↓
    CourseOfferingService
Но не сме направили общ BaseController, защото това на този етап би било излишна абстракция. Всеки Controller си остава самостоятелен и отговорен за своя ресурс.
И още нещо важно: StudentService в момента физически изтрива Student с Remove(), докато Course и CourseOffering използват soft delete. Не бих го променял автоматично сега, защото това вече е отделно архитектурно решение, което трябва да обсъдим спрямо историята и Enrollment-ите.
Направи тези два Controller-а и пусни Build. После можем да уеднаквим и service-ите, ако има смисъл, преди да започнем View-овете.

ApplicationDbContext.cs
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using StudentManagement.Models;

namespace StudentManagement.Data
{
    public class ApplicationDbContext : DbContext
    {
        public ApplicationDbContext(
            DbContextOptions<ApplicationDbContext> options)
            : base(options)
        {
        }

        public DbSet<Student> Students { get; set; } = null!;

        public DbSet<Area> Areas { get; set; } = null!;

        public DbSet<StudyProgram> StudyPrograms { get; set; } = null!;

        public DbSet<Module> Modules { get; set; } = null!;

        public DbSet<Course> Courses { get; set; } = null!;

        public DbSet<CourseOffering> CourseOfferings { get; set; } = null!;

        public DbSet<Enrollment> Enrollments { get; set; } = null!;

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
        {
            base.OnModelCreating(modelBuilder);

            foreach (var foreignKey in modelBuilder.Model
                .GetEntityTypes()
                .SelectMany(e => e.GetForeignKeys()))
            {
                foreignKey.DeleteBehavior = DeleteBehavior.Restrict;
            }

            modelBuilder.Entity<CourseOffering>()
                .ToTable("CourseOfferings", table =>
                {
                    table.HasCheckConstraint(
                        "CK_CourseOffering_EndDate_After_StartDate",
                        "[EndDate] >= [StartDate]");

                    table.HasCheckConstraint(
                        "CK_CourseOffering_EnrollmentEndDate_After_StartDate",
                        "[EnrollmentEndDate] >= [EnrollmentStartDate]");

                    table.HasCheckConstraint(
                        "CK_CourseOffering_EnrollmentStartDate_Before_StartDate",
                        "[EnrollmentStartDate] <= [StartDate]");
                });

            modelBuilder.Entity<Enrollment>()
                .HasIndex(e => new { e.StudentId, e.CourseOfferingId })
                .IsUnique();
        }
    }
}

EnrollmentStartDate <= EnrollmentEndDate

EnrollmentStartDate <= StartDate

Преди да добавяме UI, нека Service-ът поеме правилно CRUD отговорността. Ще запазим GetByIdAsync() и ще добавим GetAllAsync, CreateAsync, UpdateAsync, DeleteAsync.
Има едно важно решение: при Create и Update ще проверяваме дали свързаният Course съществува и не е soft-deleted. Така не можем да създадем CourseOffering за невалиден курс.
Готовият файл:
CourseOfferingService.cs
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using StudentManagement.Data;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

namespace StudentManagement.Services;

public class CourseOfferingService : ICourseOfferingService
{
    private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;

    public CourseOfferingService(ApplicationDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }

    public async Task<IEnumerable<CourseOffering>> GetAllAsync()
    {
        return await _context.CourseOfferings
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(co => co.Course)
            .Where(co =>
                !co.IsDeleted &&
                !co.Course.IsDeleted)
            .OrderBy(co => co.StartDate)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<CourseOffering?> GetByIdAsync(int id)
    {
        return await _context.CourseOfferings
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(co => co.Course)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co =>
                co.Id == id &&
                !co.IsDeleted &&
                !co.Course.IsDeleted);
    }

    public async Task<CourseOffering?> CreateAsync(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        var courseExists = await _context.Courses
            .AnyAsync(c =>
                c.Id == courseOffering.CourseId &&
                !c.IsDeleted);

        if (!courseExists)
        {
            return null;
        }

        courseOffering.IsDeleted = false;

        _context.CourseOfferings.Add(courseOffering);

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return courseOffering;
    }

    public async Task<bool> UpdateAsync(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        var existingCourseOffering = await _context.CourseOfferings
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co =>
                co.Id == courseOffering.Id &&
                !co.IsDeleted);

        if (existingCourseOffering == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var courseExists = await _context.Courses
            .AnyAsync(c =>
                c.Id == courseOffering.CourseId &&
                !c.IsDeleted);

        if (!courseExists)
        {
            return false;
        }

        existingCourseOffering.CourseId =
            courseOffering.CourseId;

        existingCourseOffering.StartDate =
            courseOffering.StartDate;

        existingCourseOffering.EndDate =
            courseOffering.EndDate;

        existingCourseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate =
            courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate;

        existingCourseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate =
            courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate;

        existingCourseOffering.Capacity =
            courseOffering.Capacity;

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return true;
    }

    public async Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await _context.CourseOfferings
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co =>
                co.Id == id &&
                !co.IsDeleted);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        courseOffering.IsDeleted = true;

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return true;
    }
}

Добре. Правим първо няма да слагаме проверки за дати/капацитет в Controller-а — те принадлежат на validation/service CourseOfferingsController.
Тук ще използваме ICourseOfferingService за CRUD и няма да слагаме проверки за дати/капацитет в Controller-а — те принадлежат на validation/service/database слоя.
CourseOfferingsController.cs
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

namespace StudentManagement.Controllers;

public class CourseOfferingsController : Controller
{
    private readonly ICourseOfferingService _courseOfferingService;

    public CourseOfferingsController(
        ICourseOfferingService courseOfferingService)
    {
        _courseOfferingService = courseOfferingService;
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var courseOfferings = await _courseOfferingService.GetAllAsync();

        return View(courseOfferings);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    public IActionResult Create()
    {
        return View(new CourseOffering());
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(courseOffering);
        }

        var created = await _courseOfferingService
            .CreateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (created == null)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(
                nameof(courseOffering.CourseId),
                "The selected course is not available.");

            return View(courseOffering);
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(
        int id,
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (id != courseOffering.Id)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            return View(courseOffering);
        }

        var updated = await _courseOfferingService
            .UpdateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (!updated)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id)
    {
        var deleted = await _courseOfferingService
            .DeleteAsync(id);

        if (!deleted)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }
}

CourseOfferingViewModel.cs
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using StudentManagement.Common.Constants;

namespace StudentManagement.ViewModels;

public class CourseOfferingViewModel : IValidatableObject
{
    [Required]
    public int CourseId { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime StartDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime EndDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime EnrollmentStartDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime EnrollmentEndDate { get; set; }

    [Range(1, int.MaxValue)]
    public int Capacity { get; set; }

    public IEnumerable<CourseSelectItemViewModel> Courses { get; set; }
        = new List<CourseSelectItemViewModel>();

    public IEnumerable<ValidationResult> Validate(
        ValidationContext validationContext)
    {
        if (EndDate < StartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "End date cannot be before start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EndDate) });
        }

        if (EnrollmentEndDate < EnrollmentStartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "Enrollment end date cannot be before enrollment start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EnrollmentEndDate) });
        }

        if (EnrollmentStartDate > StartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "Enrollment start date cannot be after course start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EnrollmentStartDate) });
        }
    }
}

public class CourseSelectItemViewModel
{
    public int Id { get; set; }

    public string Code { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}
Защо два ViewModel-а?
Тук имаме:
CourseOfferingViewModel
        ↓
данните на формата

CourseSelectItemViewModel
        ↓
само данните, необходими за dropdown-а
Така не подаваме целия Course entity към View-а.
И още нещо важно: не използвам CourseConstants, защото тук няма полета за Code/Name, които да валидираме. Затова няма нужда от този using.

   ↓
ViewModel → само нужните полета
   ↓
UI → dropdown

ICourseService.cs
using StudentManagement.Models;

namespace StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

public interface ICourseService
{
    Task<IEnumerable<Course>> GetAllAsync();

    Task<IEnumerable<Course>> GetAvailableForOfferingAsync();

    Task<Course?> GetByIdAsync(int id);

    Task<Course?> CreateAsync(Course course);

    Task<bool> UpdateAsync(Course course);

    Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id);
}
На този етап не добавяме параметър courseOfferingId. Всички активни Courses са допустими за избор, а конкретните бизнес ограничения ще ги добавяме там, където реално станат необходими.
След това ми изпрати текущия CourseService.cs и ще добавим само метода, без да променяме останалата логика.
1.	using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore; using StudentManagement.Data; using StudentManagement.Models; using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces; namespace StudentManagement.Services; public class CourseService : ICourseService { private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context; public CourseService(ApplicationDbContext context) { _context = context; } public async Task<IEnumerable<Course>> GetAllAsync() { return await _context.Courses .AsNoTracking() .Where(c => !c.IsDeleted) .OrderBy(c => c.Name) .ToListAsync(); } public async Task<Course?> GetByIdAsync(int id) { return await _context.Courses .AsNoTracking() .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == id && !c.IsDeleted); } public async Task<Course?> CreateAsync(Course course) { course.IsDeleted = false; _context.Courses.Add(course); await _context.SaveChangesAsync(); return course; } public async Task<bool> UpdateAsync(Course course) { var existingCourse = await _context.Courses .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == course.Id && !c.IsDeleted); if (existingCourse == null) { return false; } existingCourse.Code = course.Code; existingCourse.Name = course.Name; existingCourse.Description = course.Description; await _context.SaveChangesAsync(); return true; } public async Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id) { var course = await _context.Courses .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Id == id && !c.IsDeleted); if (course == null) { return false; } course.IsDeleted = true; await _context.SaveChangesAsync(); return true; } }

Да. Тук промяната е малка и можем да запазим останалия CourseService напълно същия.
Добавяме само GetAvailableForOfferingAsync(). Понеже на този етап условието е просто Course да е активен, методът е:
CourseService.cs
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using StudentManagement.Data;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

namespace StudentManagement.Services;

public class CourseService : ICourseService
{
    private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;

    public CourseService(ApplicationDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }

    public async Task<IEnumerable<Course>> GetAllAsync()
    {
        return await _context.Courses
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(c => !c.IsDeleted)
            .OrderBy(c => c.Name)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<IEnumerable<Course>> GetAvailableForOfferingAsync()
    {
        return await _context.Courses
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(c => !c.IsDeleted)
            .OrderBy(c => c.Name)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<Course?> GetByIdAsync(int id)
    {
        return await _context.Courses
            .AsNoTracking()
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c =>
                c.Id == id &&
                !c.IsDeleted);
    }

    public async Task<Course?> CreateAsync(Course course)
    {
        course.IsDeleted = false;

        _context.Courses.Add(course);

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return course;
    }

    public async Task<bool> UpdateAsync(Course course)
    {
        var existingCourse = await _context.Courses
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c =>
                c.Id == course.Id &&
                !c.IsDeleted);

        if (existingCourse == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        existingCourse.Code = course.Code;
        existingCourse.Name = course.Name;
        existingCourse.Description = course.Description;

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return true;
    }

    public async Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id)
    {
        var course = await _context.Courses
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c =>
                c.Id == id &&
                !c.IsDeleted);

        if (course == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        course.IsDeleted = true;

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return true;
    }
}
И да — в момента двата метода изглеждат еднакви. Това е нарочно, но не бих оставил дублираната LINQ логика така завинаги.
Причината да имаме отделен метод е архитектурната граница:
GetAllAsync()
    → Courses UI

GetAvailableForOfferingAsync()
    → CourseOffering UI
По-късно вторият може да стане например:
само активни
+ неархивирани
+ допустими за нов offering
+ други бизнес правила
без да променяме GetAllAsync().
Следващата стъпка вече е Controller-ът, където ще инжектираме и ICourseService, за да напълним CourseOfferingViewModel.Courses. Там ще направим и важното презареждане на списъка при validation error.

И ще имаме един общ метод PrepareViewModelAsync(), за да не повтаряме зареждането на Courses при Create, Edit и validation error.
CourseOfferingsController.cs
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;
using StudentManagement.ViewModels;

namespace StudentManagement.Controllers;

public class CourseOfferingsController : Controller
{
    private readonly ICourseOfferingService _courseOfferingService;
    private readonly ICourseService _courseService;

    public CourseOfferingsController(
        ICourseOfferingService courseOfferingService,
        ICourseService courseService)
    {
        _courseOfferingService = courseOfferingService;
        _courseService = courseService;
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var courseOfferings = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetAllAsync();

        return View(courseOfferings);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Create()
    {
        var viewModel = new CourseOfferingViewModel();

        await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

        return View(viewModel);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

            return View(viewModel);
        }

        var courseOffering = new CourseOffering
        {
            CourseId = viewModel.CourseId,
            StartDate = viewModel.StartDate,
            EndDate = viewModel.EndDate,
            EnrollmentStartDate = viewModel.EnrollmentStartDate,
            EnrollmentEndDate = viewModel.EnrollmentEndDate,
            Capacity = viewModel.Capacity
        };

        var created = await _courseOfferingService
            .CreateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (created == null)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(
                nameof(viewModel.CourseId),
                "The selected course is not available.");

            await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

            return View(viewModel);
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var viewModel = new CourseOfferingViewModel
        {
            CourseId = courseOffering.CourseId,
            StartDate = courseOffering.StartDate,
            EndDate = courseOffering.EndDate,
            EnrollmentStartDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate,
            EnrollmentEndDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate,
            Capacity = courseOffering.Capacity
        };

        await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

        return View(viewModel);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(
        int id,
        CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

            return View(viewModel);
        }

        var courseOffering = new CourseOffering
        {
            Id = id,
            CourseId = viewModel.CourseId,
            StartDate = viewModel.StartDate,
            EndDate = viewModel.EndDate,
            EnrollmentStartDate = viewModel.EnrollmentStartDate,
            EnrollmentEndDate = viewModel.EnrollmentEndDate,
            Capacity = viewModel.Capacity
        };

        var updated = await _courseOfferingService
            .UpdateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (!updated)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var courseOffering = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id)
    {
        var deleted = await _courseOfferingService
            .DeleteAsync(id);

        if (!deleted)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task PrepareViewModelAsync(
        CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel)
    {
        var courses = await _courseService
            .GetAvailableForOfferingAsync();

        viewModel.Courses = courses
            .Select(c => new CourseSelectItemViewModel
            {
                Id = c.Id,
                Code = c.Code,
                Name = c.Name
            })
            .ToList();
    }
}
Защо това е по-добре
Вече Controller-ът не съдържа LINQ към базата за Courses.
Получаваме:
Database
   ↓
CourseService
   ↓
само активни Courses
   ↓
Controller
   ↓
CourseSelectItemViewModel
   ↓
UI dropdown
А PrepareViewModelAsync() премахва повторението при:
o	Create GET
o	Create POST при грешка
o	Edit GET
o	Edit POST при грешка
Това е точно консистентността, която търсихме по-рано.
Сега пусни Build. Ако е успешен, следващата стъпка е да направим Views/CourseOfferings/Create.cshtml и Edit.cshtml.

И ще продължим по този начин: ако видя повторение или архитектурен проблем, ще ти казвам защо го променяме, а не просто ще ти давам готов код. Така постепенно ще започнеш сам да ги хващаш.
А за проекта държим линията:
o	Controller → HTTP/UI flow
o	ViewModel → какво получава и изпраща UI
o	Service → бизнес логика
o	DbContext → persistence и DB constraints
o	Model → структура + model validation
o	Common → общи enum-и, constants, messages и т.н.
o	Database → последната защита на целостта
Това е доста добра основа за проекта ти.

CourseOfferingsController.cs
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;
using StudentManagement.ViewModels;

namespace StudentManagement.Controllers;

public class CourseOfferingsController : Controller
{
    private readonly ICourseOfferingService _courseOfferingService;
    private readonly ICourseService _courseService;

    public CourseOfferingsController(
        ICourseOfferingService courseOfferingService,
        ICourseService courseService)
    {
        _courseOfferingService = courseOfferingService;
        _courseService = courseService;
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Index()
    {
        var courseOfferings = await _courseOfferingService
            .GetAllAsync();

        return View(courseOfferings);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Details(int? id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Create()
    {
        var viewModel = new CourseOfferingViewModel();

        await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

        return View(viewModel);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Create(
        CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

            return View(viewModel);
        }

        var courseOffering = new CourseOffering
        {
            CourseId = viewModel.CourseId,
            StartDate = viewModel.StartDate,
            EndDate = viewModel.EndDate,
            EnrollmentStartDate = viewModel.EnrollmentStartDate,
            EnrollmentEndDate = viewModel.EnrollmentEndDate,
            Capacity = viewModel.Capacity
        };

        var created = await _courseOfferingService
            .CreateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (created == null)
        {
            ModelState.AddModelError(
                nameof(viewModel.CourseId),
                "The selected course is not available.");

            await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

            return View(viewModel);
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        var viewModel = new CourseOfferingViewModel
        {
            CourseId = courseOffering.CourseId,
            StartDate = courseOffering.StartDate,
            EndDate = courseOffering.EndDate,
            EnrollmentStartDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate,
            EnrollmentEndDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate,
            Capacity = courseOffering.Capacity
        };

        await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

        return View(viewModel);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(
        int id,
        CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel)
    {
        if (!ModelState.IsValid)
        {
            await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel);

            return View(viewModel);
        }

        var courseOffering = new CourseOffering
        {
            Id = id,
            CourseId = viewModel.CourseId,
            StartDate = viewModel.StartDate,
            EndDate = viewModel.EndDate,
            EnrollmentStartDate = viewModel.EnrollmentStartDate,
            EnrollmentEndDate = viewModel.EnrollmentEndDate,
            Capacity = viewModel.Capacity
        };

        var updated = await _courseOfferingService
            .UpdateAsync(courseOffering);

        if (!updated)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return View(courseOffering);
    }

    [HttpPost]
    [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id)
    {
        var deleted = await _courseOfferingService
            .DeleteAsync(id);

        if (!deleted)
        {
            return NotFound();
        }

        return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index));
    }

    private async Task<CourseOffering?> GetCourseOfferingAsync(int? id)
    {
        if (id == null)
        {
            return null;
        }

        return await _courseOfferingService
            .GetByIdAsync(id.Value);
    }

    private async Task PrepareViewModelAsync(
        CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel)
    {
        var courses = await _courseService
            .GetAvailableForOfferingAsync();

        viewModel.Courses = courses
            .Select(c => new CourseSelectItemViewModel
            {
                Id = c.Id,
                Code = c.Code,
                Name = c.Name
            })
            .ToList();
    }
}
Какво вече е консистентно
С StudentsController имаме еднакъв pattern:
Details/Edit/Delete
        ↓
GetEntityAsync(id)
        ↓
null?
        ↓
NotFound()
А специфичното за CourseOffering остава отделно:
PrepareViewModelAsync()
        ↓
CourseService
        ↓
само допустими Courses
Това е правилното място за него, защото StudentController няма dropdown с външна зависимост, докато CourseOffering има.

CourseOfferingViewModel.cs
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace StudentManagement.ViewModels;

public class CourseOfferingViewModel : IValidatableObject
{
    [Required]
    public int CourseId { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime StartDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime EndDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime EnrollmentStartDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime EnrollmentEndDate { get; set; }

    [Range(1, int.MaxValue)]
    public int Capacity { get; set; }

    public IEnumerable<CourseSelectItemViewModel> Courses { get; set; }
        = new List<CourseSelectItemViewModel>();

    public IEnumerable<ValidationResult> Validate(
        ValidationContext validationContext)
    {
        if (EndDate < StartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "End date cannot be before start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EndDate) });
        }

        if (EnrollmentEndDate < EnrollmentStartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "Enrollment end date cannot be before enrollment start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EnrollmentEndDate) });
        }

        if (EnrollmentStartDate > StartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "Enrollment start date cannot be after course start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EnrollmentStartDate) });
        }
    }
}

public class CourseSelectItemViewModel
{
    public int Id { get; set; }

    public string Code { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}
И тук вече имаме хубаво разделение:
o	CourseOffering → domain model + DB validation.
o	CourseOfferingViewModel → validation за входа от UI.
o	CourseSelectItemViewModel → само данните за dropdown-а.
o	CourseOfferingService → бизнес операции.
o	CourseService → кои Courses са допустими за избор.

А текущият ти service, който ми показа по-рано, има само GetByIdAsync().
Не добавяй нищо на сляпо. Изпрати ми текущите:
o	ICourseOfferingService.cs
o	CourseOfferingService.cs
и ще ги приведем към същия pattern, който вече използваме при StudentService и CourseService, включително soft-delete и validation правилата.
1.	using StudentManagement.Models; namespace StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces; public interface ICourseOfferingService { Task<IEnumerable<CourseOffering>> GetAllAsync(); Task<CourseOffering?> GetByIdAsync(int id); Task<CourseOffering?> CreateAsync( CourseOffering courseOffering); Task<bool> UpdateAsync( CourseOffering courseOffering); Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id); } и using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore; using StudentManagement.Data; using StudentManagement.Models; using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces; namespace StudentManagement.Services; public class CourseOfferingService : ICourseOfferingService { private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context; public CourseOfferingService(ApplicationDbContext context) { _context = context; } public async Task<IEnumerable<CourseOffering>> GetAllAsync() { return await _context.CourseOfferings .AsNoTracking() .Include(co => co.Course) .Where(co => !co.IsDeleted && !co.Course.IsDeleted) .OrderBy(co => co.StartDate) .ToListAsync(); } public async Task<CourseOffering?> GetByIdAsync(int id) { return await _context.CourseOfferings .AsNoTracking() .Include(co => co.Course) .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co => co.Id == id && !co.IsDeleted && !co.Course.IsDeleted); } public async Task<CourseOffering?> CreateAsync( CourseOffering courseOffering) { var courseExists = await _context.Courses .AnyAsync(c => c.Id == courseOffering.CourseId && !c.IsDeleted); if (!courseExists) { return null; } courseOffering.IsDeleted = false; _context.CourseOfferings.Add(courseOffering); await _context.SaveChangesAsync(); return courseOffering; } public async Task<bool> UpdateAsync( CourseOffering courseOffering) { var existingCourseOffering = await _context.CourseOfferings .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co => co.Id == courseOffering.Id && !co.IsDeleted); if (existingCourseOffering == null) { return false; } var courseExists = await _context.Courses .AnyAsync(c => c.Id == courseOffering.CourseId && !c.IsDeleted); if (!courseExists) { return false; } existingCourseOffering.CourseId = courseOffering.CourseId; existingCourseOffering.StartDate = courseOffering.StartDate; existingCourseOffering.EndDate = courseOffering.EndDate; existingCourseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate; existingCourseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate; existingCourseOffering.Capacity = courseOffering.Capacity; await _context.SaveChangesAsync(); return true; } public async Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id) { var courseOffering = await _context.CourseOfferings .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co => co.Id == id && !co.IsDeleted); Има обаче едно важно нещо, което трябва да добавим сега: новите бизнес правила за датите трябва да бъдат защитени и на service ниво, а не да разчитаме само на UI/ViewModel.
В момента имаме:
o	ViewModel validation → защита на входа от UI;
o	Model IValidatableObject → domain/model validation;
o	DB constraints → защита на базата;
o	но Service може да бъде извикан и от друго място, без ViewModel.
Така че Service трябва да пази бизнес операцията.

using StudentManagement.Models;

namespace StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

{

    Task<CourseOffering?> GetByIdAsync(int id);

    Task<bool> UpdateAsync(

2. Добавяме validation в CourseOfferingService
При CreateAsync() и UpdateAsync() проверяваме:

Това са бизнес правила, които вече сме избрали.
Готовият service:
CourseOfferingService.cs
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using StudentManagement.Data;
using StudentManagement.Models;
using StudentManagement.Services.Interfaces;

namespace StudentManagement.Services;

public class CourseOfferingService : ICourseOfferingService
{
    private readonly ApplicationDbContext _context;

    public CourseOfferingService(ApplicationDbContext context)
    {
        _context = context;
    }

    public async Task<IEnumerable<CourseOffering>> GetAllAsync()
    {
        return await _context.CourseOfferings
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(co => co.Course)
            .Where(co =>
                !co.IsDeleted &&
                !co.Course.IsDeleted)
            .OrderBy(co => co.StartDate)
            .ToListAsync();
    }

    public async Task<CourseOffering?> GetByIdAsync(int id)
    {
        return await _context.CourseOfferings
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Include(co => co.Course)
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co =>
                co.Id == id &&
                !co.IsDeleted &&
                !co.Course.IsDeleted);
    }

    public async Task<CourseOffering?> CreateAsync(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (!IsValidDates(courseOffering))
        {
            return null;
        }

        var courseExists = await _context.Courses
            .AnyAsync(c =>
                c.Id == courseOffering.CourseId &&
                !c.IsDeleted);

        if (!courseExists)
        {
            return null;
        }

        courseOffering.IsDeleted = false;

        _context.CourseOfferings.Add(courseOffering);

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return courseOffering;
    }

    public async Task<bool> UpdateAsync(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        if (!IsValidDates(courseOffering))
        {
            return false;
        }

        var existingCourseOffering = await _context.CourseOfferings
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co =>
                co.Id == courseOffering.Id &&
                !co.IsDeleted);

        if (existingCourseOffering == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        var courseExists = await _context.Courses
            .AnyAsync(c =>
                c.Id == courseOffering.CourseId &&
                !c.IsDeleted);

        if (!courseExists)
        {
            return false;
        }

        existingCourseOffering.CourseId =
            courseOffering.CourseId;

        existingCourseOffering.StartDate =
            courseOffering.StartDate;

        existingCourseOffering.EndDate =
            courseOffering.EndDate;

        existingCourseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate =
            courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate;

        existingCourseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate =
            courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate;

        existingCourseOffering.Capacity =
            courseOffering.Capacity;

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return true;
    }

    public async Task<bool> DeleteAsync(int id)
    {
        var courseOffering = await _context.CourseOfferings
            .FirstOrDefaultAsync(co =>
                co.Id == id &&
                !co.IsDeleted);

        if (courseOffering == null)
        {
            return false;
        }

        courseOffering.IsDeleted = true;

        await _context.SaveChangesAsync();

        return true;
    }

    private static bool IsValidDates(
        CourseOffering courseOffering)
    {
        return courseOffering.StartDate <= courseOffering.EndDate
            && courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate
                <= courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate
            && courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate
                <= courseOffering.StartDate;
    }
}
Защо го правим и тук?
Получаваме няколко защитни слоя:
UI
 ↓
CourseOfferingViewModel
 ↓
Controller
 ↓
CourseOfferingService
 ↓
EF Core / Model
 ↓
Database constraints
 ↓
Database
Това напълно съответства на принципа, който избра още в началото: всеки слой да има собствена отговорност и да пази целостта на системата.
И още нещо важно: не добавяме DateTime.Now/DateTime.UtcNow проверки за CourseOffering датите на този етап. За момента сме договорили само относителните правила между датите. Ако по-късно решим, че например enrollment не може да се създава след EnrollmentEndDate, това е отделно правило и ще го добавим там, където реално принадлежи — при Enrollment workflow-а.

id) { var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id); if (courseOffering == null) { return NotFound(); } return View(courseOffering); } public async Task<IActionResult> Create() { var viewModel = new CourseOfferingViewModel(); await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel); return View(viewModel); } [HttpPost] [ValidateAntiForgeryToken] public async Task<IActionResult> Create( CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel) { if (!ModelState.IsValid) { await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel); return View(viewModel); } var courseOffering = new CourseOffering { CourseId = viewModel.CourseId, StartDate = viewModel.StartDate, EndDate = viewModel.EndDate, EnrollmentStartDate = viewModel.EnrollmentStartDate, EnrollmentEndDate = viewModel.EnrollmentEndDate, Capacity = viewModel.Capacity }; var created = await _courseOfferingService .CreateAsync(courseOffering); if (created == null) { ModelState.AddModelError( nameof(viewModel.CourseId), "The selected course is not available."); await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel); return View(viewModel); } return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index)); } public async Task<IActionResult> Edit(int? id) { var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id); if (courseOffering == null) { return NotFound(); } var viewModel = new CourseOfferingViewModel { CourseId = courseOffering.CourseId, StartDate = courseOffering.StartDate, EndDate = courseOffering.EndDate, EnrollmentStartDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate, EnrollmentEndDate = courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate, Capacity = courseOffering.Capacity }; await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel); return View(viewModel); } [HttpPost] [ValidateAntiForgeryToken] public async Task<IActionResult> Edit( int id, CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel) { if (!ModelState.IsValid) { await PrepareViewModelAsync(viewModel); return View(viewModel); } var courseOffering = new CourseOffering { Id = id, CourseId = viewModel.CourseId, StartDate = viewModel.StartDate, EndDate = viewModel.EndDate, EnrollmentStartDate = viewModel.EnrollmentStartDate, EnrollmentEndDate = viewModel.EnrollmentEndDate, Capacity = viewModel.Capacity }; var updated = await _courseOfferingService .UpdateAsync(courseOffering); if (!updated) { return NotFound(); } return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index)); } public async Task<IActionResult> Delete(int? id) { var courseOffering = await GetCourseOfferingAsync(id); if (courseOffering == null) { return NotFound(); } return View(courseOffering); } [HttpPost] [ValidateAntiForgeryToken] public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteConfirmed(int id) { var deleted = await _courseOfferingService .DeleteAsync(id); if (!deleted) { return NotFound(); } return RedirectToAction(nameof(Index)); } private async Task<CourseOffering?> GetCourseOfferingAsync(int? id) { if (id == null) { return null; } return await _courseOfferingService .GetByIdAsync(id.Value); } private async Task PrepareViewModelAsync( CourseOfferingViewModel viewModel) { var courses = await _courseService .GetAvailableForOfferingAsync(); viewModel.Courses = courses .Select(c => new CourseSelectItemViewModel { Id = c.Id, Code = c.Code, Name = c.Name }) .ToList(); } }

Views/CourseOfferings/Index.cshtml
@model IEnumerable<StudentManagement.Models.CourseOffering>

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Course Offerings";
}

<h1>Course Offerings</h1>

<a asp-action="Create"
   class="btn btn-primary">
    Add Course Offering
</a>

@{
    var number = 1;
}

<table class="table">
    <thead>
        <tr>
            <th>№</th>
            <th>Course</th>
            <th>Start Date</th>
            <th>End Date</th>
            <th>Enrollment Start Date</th>
            <th>Enrollment End Date</th>
            <th>Capacity</th>
            <th>Actions</th>
        </tr>
    </thead>

    <tbody>
        @foreach (var courseOffering in Model)
        {
            <tr>
                <td>@number</td>

                <td>
                    @courseOffering.Course.Code -
                    @courseOffering.Course.Name
                </td>

                <td>
                    @courseOffering.StartDate.ToShortDateString()
                </td>

                <td>
                    @courseOffering.EndDate.ToShortDateString()
                </td>

                <td>
                    @courseOffering.EnrollmentStartDate.ToShortDateString()
                </td>

                <td>
                    @courseOffering.EnrollmentEndDate.ToShortDateString()
                </td>

                <td>
                    @courseOffering.Capacity
                </td>

                <td>
                    <a asp-action="Details"
                       asp-route-id="@courseOffering.Id"
                       class="btn btn-info">
                        Details
                    </a>

                    <a asp-action="Edit"
                       asp-route-id="@courseOffering.Id"
                       class="btn btn-warning">
                        Edit
                    </a>

                    <a asp-action="Delete"
                       asp-route-id="@courseOffering.Id"
                       class="btn btn-danger">
                        Delete
                    </a>
                </td>
            </tr>

            number++;
        }
    </tbody>
</table>
Това е нарочно почти 1:1 с Courses/Index.cshtml като структура:
Courses
 ├── Add
 ├── №
 ├── данни
 └── Details / Edit / Delete
и:
Course Offerings
 ├── Add
 ├── №
 ├── Course
 ├── Course dates
 ├── Enrollment dates
 ├── Capacity
 └── Details / Edit / Delete
След това добавяме само Course Offerings в sidebar-а и вече ще можеш да стигнеш до Create през UI.

Да — много правилно го забелязваш. Тук всъщност имаме две различни неща, които не трябва да смесваме.
1. Course няма Start/End Date — и това е правилно
Course описва самия курс като предмет/обучение:
Code
Name
Description
IsDeleted
Например:
C#101
Introduction to C#
Basic C# programming
Той не е конкретно провеждане.
2. CourseOffering е конкретното провеждане
Там вече имаме:
Course
StartDate
EndDate
EnrollmentStartDate
EnrollmentEndDate
Capacity
Например:
C#101 - Introduction to C#

Course Offering:
Start: 01.11.2026
End:   30.11.2026

Enrollment:
Start: 01.10.2026
End:   25.10.2026

Capacity: 20
Така един Course може да има много CourseOfferings.
________________________________________
А за IsDeleted
Тук също си прав — в момента имаме IsDeleted в Course, но не ти трябва отделно поле в UI за него.
Това:
public bool IsDeleted { get; set; }
е системно поле, а не потребителско поле.
Затова не правим:
☐ Is Deleted
в Create/Edit Course.
Вече имаме:
Courses
   ↓
Delete
   ↓
CourseService.DeleteAsync()
   ↓
course.IsDeleted = true
И след това GetAllAsync() прави:
.Where(c => !c.IsDeleted)
Точно това е soft-delete pattern-ът, който избрахме.
________________________________________
Но има един реален проблем
В CourseOfferingService имаме:
.Where(co =>
    !co.IsDeleted &&
    !co.Course.IsDeleted)
Това означава:
Ако Course бъде soft-deleted, неговите CourseOfferings престават да се показват.
Но самите CourseOfferings не се изтриват.
Това всъщност е хубаво за историята/статистиката, която искаме да пазим.
Получаваме:
Course
 └── IsDeleted = true

CourseOffering
 └── IsDeleted = false
Историята остава в DB, но нормалният UI не я показва.
________________________________________
И затова аз не бих променял Course сега.
Не му добавяме Start/End Date.
Не добавяме IsDeleted checkbox.
Не добавяме допълнителна функционалност само заради това.
Course = какво се преподава.
CourseOffering = кога и как конкретно се провежда.
Enrollment = кой се записва за конкретно CourseOffering.

Създай:
Views/CourseOfferings/Delete.cshtml
Views/CourseOfferings/Delete.cshtml
@model StudentManagement.Models.CourseOffering

@{
    ViewData["Title"] = "Delete Course Offering";
}

<h1>Delete Course Offering</h1>

<h3>Are you sure you want to delete this course offering?</h3>

<div>
    <h4>@Model.Course.Name</h4>

    <dl>
        <dt>Course</dt>
        <dd>
            @Model.Course.Code - @Model.Course.Name
        </dd>

        <dt>Start Date</dt>
        <dd>@Model.StartDate.ToShortDateString()</dd>

        <dt>End Date</dt>
        <dd>@Model.EndDate.ToShortDateString()</dd>

        <dt>Enrollment Start Date</dt>
        <dd>@Model.EnrollmentStartDate.ToShortDateString()</dd>

        <dt>Enrollment End Date</dt>
        <dd>@Model.EnrollmentEndDate.ToShortDateString()</dd>

        <dt>Capacity</dt>
        <dd>@Model.Capacity</dd>
    </dl>
</div>

<form asp-action="DeleteConfirmed" method="post">

    <input type="hidden"
           name="id"
           value="@Model.Id" />

    <button type="submit"
            class="btn btn-danger">
        Delete
    </button>

    <a asp-action="Index"
       class="btn btn-secondary">
        Cancel
    </a>

</form>
Тук използваме същия подход като при Course:

  ↓
Delete
  ↓
Delete.cshtml — потвърждение
  ↓
DeleteConfirmed

CourseOfferingService.DeleteAsync()

IsDeleted = true

Да — точно тук са полетата, които трябва да променим. 👍
И [Required] остава. Променяме само типа от DateTime на DateTime?.
Причината е:
o	DateTime → винаги има стойност, дори да е 01.01.0001.
o	DateTime? → може да бъде null.
o	[Required] → тогава реално може да каже „полето е задължително“.
Но понеже имаме и Validate(), трябва да го направим безопасен за null.
Ето целия готов CourseOfferingViewModel.cs, за да не променяме отделни части:
CourseOfferingViewModel.cs
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;

namespace StudentManagement.ViewModels;

public class CourseOfferingViewModel : IValidatableObject
{
    [Required]
    public int CourseId { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime? StartDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime? EndDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime? EnrollmentStartDate { get; set; }

    [Required]
    public DateTime? EnrollmentEndDate { get; set; }

    [Range(1, int.MaxValue)]
    public int Capacity { get; set; }

    public IEnumerable<CourseSelectItemViewModel> Courses { get; set; }
        = new List<CourseSelectItemViewModel>();

    public IEnumerable<ValidationResult> Validate(
        ValidationContext validationContext)
    {
        if (StartDate.HasValue &&
            EndDate.HasValue &&
            EndDate < StartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "End date cannot be before start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EndDate) });
        }

        if (EnrollmentStartDate.HasValue &&
            EnrollmentEndDate.HasValue &&
            EnrollmentEndDate < EnrollmentStartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "Enrollment end date cannot be before enrollment start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EnrollmentEndDate) });
        }

        if (EnrollmentStartDate.HasValue &&
            StartDate.HasValue &&
            EnrollmentStartDate > StartDate)
        {
            yield return new ValidationResult(
                "Enrollment start date cannot be after course start date.",
                new[] { nameof(EnrollmentStartDate) });
        }
    }
}

public class CourseSelectItemViewModel
{
    public int Id { get; set; }

    public string Code { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
}
